Next is piece 22: a Users page for admins to create accounts, change roles, deactivate people, and reset passwords, plus an audit trail that records who changed what.

go next while I do this one.

Steps left: 8 pieces (22 to 29), counting this one. I split the old "users and audit" piece in two, and put the audit trail first so that every user action is recorded from the day user management exists:

22. Audit trail: who changed what, and when (this piece)
23. User management page
24. Edit locks with admin override
25. Personalization
26. Dashboard
27. CSV import and export
28. Hardening, polish, and a full check against your original requirements
29. Running it for real
Piece 22: the audit trail
This needs pieces 20 and 21 finished, since it records who did each thing. Every successful change gets a row saying who, when, what, and the details. Admins get a page to browse it.

What gets recorded

Scores: every changed cell, with the student, the parameter, the old value, and the new one. This is the most important part, and it's saved in the same database transaction as the scores, so it can't get out of step.
Everything else (students, parameters, parameter changes, the start date, and sign-ins including failed ones): a middleware writes a row after each successful change. Passwords are never logged, and any field named like a password or token is blanked.
Any other successful change gets a generic entry, so nothing can slip through unlogged.
Honest limits

For the middleware-logged changes, the row is written just after the change succeeds. If the database failed in that instant, an entry could be missing, and an error prints in the server console.
The log is append-only by convention: our code never edits or deletes rows. Someone with direct database access could. Real tamper-proofing would need a separate database account for the log.
The log contains student names, so treat it like the rest of the student data.
Server part
cd /data/projects/student-panel/server
A. The table

Create src/db/migrations/006_create_audit_log.sql:

sql
CREATE TABLE audit_log (
  id          bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
  occurred_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  user_id     uuid REFERENCES users (id),
  actor       text NOT NULL,
  action      text NOT NULL,
  entity_type text,
  entity_id   text,
  details     jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
);

CREATE INDEX audit_log_occurred_idx ON audit_log (occurred_at DESC, id DESC);
CREATE INDEX audit_log_user_idx ON audit_log (user_id);
CREATE INDEX audit_log_entity_idx ON audit_log (entity_type, entity_id);
actor keeps the username as text, so a row stays readable on its own. user_id links to the account. user_id is empty for a failed sign-in.
details is jsonb, a flexible block of data the database can also search.
npm run migrate
You should see Applied 006_create_audit_log.sql.

B. The writing function

Create src/lib/audit.js:

js
export async function recordAudit(
  db,
  { userId = null, actor, action, entityType = null, entityId = null, details = {} }
) {
  await db.query(
    `INSERT INTO audit_log (user_id, actor, action, entity_type, entity_id, details)
     VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6)`,
    [userId, actor, action, entityType, entityId, JSON.stringify(details)]
  );
}
db can be the normal pool or a transaction's client. The score saving passes its client, so the log row commits or rolls back together with the scores.

C. The rules, kept pure so we can test them

Create src/lib/auditRules.js:

js
import { isUuid } from './ids.js';

const MAX_JSON_LENGTH = 2000;
const SECRET_KEY = /pass|secret|token|hash/i;

const RULES = [
  { method: 'POST', pattern: /^\/api\/students$/, action: 'student.create', entityType: 'student' },
  { method: 'PATCH', pattern: /^\/api\/students\/[^/]+$/, action: 'student.update', entityType: 'student' },
  { method: 'DELETE', pattern: /^\/api\/students\/[^/]+$/, action: 'student.delete', entityType: 'student' },
  { method: 'POST', pattern: /^\/api\/parameters$/, action: 'parameter.create', entityType: 'parameter' },
  { method: 'PATCH', pattern: /^\/api\/parameters\/[^/]+$/, action: 'parameter.update', entityType: 'parameter' },
  { method: 'POST', pattern: /^\/api\/parameter-changes$/, action: 'parameter_change.create', entityType: 'parameter_change' },
  { method: 'DELETE', pattern: /^\/api\/parameter-changes\/[^/]+$/, action: 'parameter_change.delete', entityType: 'parameter_change' },
  { method: 'PUT', pattern: /^\/api\/settings\/period-anchor$/, action: 'settings.period_anchor', entityType: 'setting' },
  { method: 'POST', pattern: /^\/api\/auth\/logout$/, action: 'auth.logout', entityType: 'user' },
  { method: 'POST', pattern: /^\/api\/auth\/change-password$/, action: 'auth.password_change', entityType: 'user' },
];

const RECORDED_BY_THEIR_OWN_ROUTES = [
  /^\/api\/evaluations\/bulk$/,
  /^\/api\/users(\/|$)/,
];

export function normalisePath(url) {
  return url.split('?')[0].replace(/\/+$/, '').toLowerCase();
}

export function classifyRequest(method, path, status) {
  if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/auth/login') {
    if (status === 200) return { action: 'auth.login', entityType: 'user' };
    if (status === 401) return { action: 'auth.login_failed', entityType: 'user' };
    return null;
  }
  if (status < 200 || status >= 300) return null;
  if (RECORDED_BY_THEIR_OWN_ROUTES.some((pattern) => pattern.test(path))) {
    return null;
  }

  const rule = RULES.find(
    (item) => item.method === method && item.pattern.test(path)
  );
  if (rule) return { action: rule.action, entityType: rule.entityType };
  return { action: 'api.change', entityType: null };
}

function redact(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(redact);
  if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value).map(([key, inner]) => [
        key,
        SECRET_KEY.test(key) ? '[hidden]' : redact(inner),
      ])
    );
  }
  return value;
}

export function safeBody(value) {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return null;
  const cleaned = redact(value);
  return JSON.stringify(cleaned).length > MAX_JSON_LENGTH
    ? { truncated: true }
    : cleaned;
}

export function entityIdFromPath(path) {
  const last = path.split('/').pop();
  return isUuid(last) ? last : null;
}
normalisePath lowercases and drops a trailing slash and the ?... part, because Express treats /API/Students/ as the same address as /api/students. Matching on the raw text could let a change slip past the log.
classifyRequest names the request and decides whether to record it. Failures (400, 403, ...) are ignored, except failed sign-ins. Anything unrecognized that succeeded is still logged as api.change. Score saves and (later) user changes log themselves, so they're skipped here to avoid double entries.
safeBody blanks fields that look like secrets and drops very large bodies.
Create test/audit.test.js:

js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  classifyRequest,
  entityIdFromPath,
  normalisePath,
  safeBody,
} from '../src/lib/auditRules.js';

test('paths are normalised before matching', () => {
  assert.equal(normalisePath('/API/Students/?page=2'), '/api/students');
  assert.equal(normalisePath('/api/students'), '/api/students');
});

test('known changes get friendly action names', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(classifyRequest('POST', '/api/students', 201), {
    action: 'student.create',
    entityType: 'student',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(classifyRequest('DELETE', '/api/students/abc', 204), {
    action: 'student.delete',
    entityType: 'student',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(classifyRequest('PUT', '/api/settings/period-anchor', 200), {
    action: 'settings.period_anchor',
    entityType: 'setting',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(classifyRequest('POST', '/api/auth/logout', 204), {
    action: 'auth.logout',
    entityType: 'user',
  });
  assert.deepEqual(classifyRequest('POST', '/api/auth/change-password', 200), {
    action: 'auth.password_change',
    entityType: 'user',
  });
});

test('sign-in outcomes are recorded, other failures are not', () => {
  assert.equal(classifyRequest('POST', '/api/auth/login', 200).action, 'auth.login');
  assert.equal(
    classifyRequest('POST', '/api/auth/login', 401).action,
    'auth.login_failed'
  );
  assert.equal(classifyRequest('POST', '/api/auth/login', 429), null);
  assert.equal(classifyRequest('POST', '/api/students', 400), null);
  assert.equal(classifyRequest('PATCH', '/api/students/abc', 403), null);
});

test('unknown changes are still recorded; self-logged ones are skipped', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(classifyRequest('POST', '/api/something-new', 200), {
    action: 'api.change',
    entityType: null,
  });
  assert.equal(classifyRequest('PUT', '/api/evaluations/bulk', 200), null);
  assert.equal(classifyRequest('POST', '/api/users', 201), null);
  assert.equal(classifyRequest('PATCH', '/api/users/abc', 200), null);
});

test('request bodies are redacted and size-limited', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(
    safeBody({ name: 'Speed', password: 'x', nested: { token: 'y', ok: 1 } }),
    { name: 'Speed', password: '[hidden]', nested: { token: '[hidden]', ok: 1 } }
  );
  assert.deepEqual(safeBody({ text: 'x'.repeat(3000) }), { truncated: true });
  assert.equal(safeBody('a string'), null);
  assert.equal(safeBody(null), null);
});

test('entity ids come from the last path segment when it is a UUID', () => {
  const id = '3f2b8c1e-4d5a-4b6c-8d7e-9f0a1b2c3d4e';
  assert.equal(entityIdFromPath(`/api/students/${id}`), id);
  assert.equal(entityIdFromPath('/api/students'), null);
});
npm test
You should see 6 new green tests, for a total of pass 42 and fail 0 (36 + 6). If any fail, send me the output before continuing.

D. The middleware

Create src/middleware/audit.js:

js
import pool from '../db/pool.js';
import { recordAudit } from '../lib/audit.js';
import {
  classifyRequest,
  entityIdFromPath,
  normalisePath,
  safeBody,
} from '../lib/auditRules.js';

const READ_ONLY = new Set(['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS']);

export function auditRequests(req, res, next) {
  if (READ_ONLY.has(req.method)) return next();

  let responseBody;
  const originalJson = res.json.bind(res);
  res.json = (body) => {
    responseBody = body;
    return originalJson(body);
  };

  res.on('finish', () => {
    const path = normalisePath(req.originalUrl);
    const kind = classifyRequest(req.method, path, res.statusCode);
    if (!kind) return;

    const isAuth = path.startsWith('/api/auth/');
    const user = req.user ?? responseBody?.user ?? null;
    const attempted =
      typeof req.body?.username === 'string'
        ? req.body.username.trim().toLowerCase().slice(0, 64)
        : null;

    const details = isAuth
      ? {
          ip: req.ip,
          ...(kind.action.startsWith('auth.login')
            ? { attemptedUsername: attempted }
            : {}),
        }
      : {
          method: req.method,
          path,
          request: safeBody(req.body),
          result: safeBody(responseBody),
        };

    recordAudit(pool, {
      userId: user?.id ?? null,
      actor: user?.username ?? attempted ?? 'unknown',
      action: kind.action,
      entityType: kind.entityType,
      entityId: isAuth
        ? (user?.id ?? null)
        : (responseBody?.id ?? entityIdFromPath(path)),
      details,
    }).catch((error) => {
      console.error('Could not write audit entry:', error);
    });
  });

  next();
}
It wraps res.json to remember what was sent back (useful for creates, where the new record's ID and code only exist in the answer).
It decides after the response finishes ('finish'), so it knows the final status.
For anything under /api/auth/ it never stores the request body, only the attempted username on sign-in events and the IP address.
E. Scores log themselves, in the same transaction

Replace all of src/routes/evaluations.js with:

js
import { Router } from 'express';
import pool from '../db/pool.js';
import { recordAudit } from '../lib/audit.js';
import { isUuid } from '../lib/ids.js';
import { periodForDate, todayIso } from '../lib/periods.js';
import {
  MAX_PERIOD,
  isValidPeriodNumber,
  parametersUsedInPeriod,
} from '../lib/parameterUse.js';
import { findProblems, validateShape } from '../lib/evaluationCheck.js';

const router = Router();

router.get('/evaluations', async (req, res) => {
  const { studentId, parameterId, periodNumber } = req.query;
  const conditions = ['value IS NOT NULL'];
  const values = [];

  if (studentId !== undefined) {
    if (!isUuid(studentId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'studentId must be a valid ID.' });
    }
    values.push(studentId);
    conditions.push(`student_id = $${values.length}`);
  }
  if (parameterId !== undefined) {
    if (!isUuid(parameterId)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'parameterId must be a valid ID.' });
    }
    values.push(parameterId);
    conditions.push(`parameter_id = $${values.length}`);
  }
  if (periodNumber !== undefined) {
    const isDigits =
      typeof periodNumber === 'string' && /^\d+$/.test(periodNumber);
    if (!isDigits || !isValidPeriodNumber(Number(periodNumber))) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        error: `periodNumber must be a whole number from 1 to ${MAX_PERIOD}.`,
      });
    }
    values.push(Number(periodNumber));
    conditions.push(`period_number = $${values.length}`);
  }

  if (values.length === 0) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      error: 'Add at least one filter: studentId, parameterId or periodNumber.',
    });
  }

  const result = await pool.query(
    `SELECT student_id    AS "studentId",
            parameter_id  AS "parameterId",
            period_number AS "periodNumber",
            value::float8 AS value,
            updated_at    AS "updatedAt"
     FROM evaluations
     WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}
     ORDER BY period_number, student_id, parameter_id
     LIMIT 20000`,
    values
  );
  res.json({ evaluations: result.rows });
});

router.put('/evaluations/bulk', async (req, res) => {
  const { periodNumber, entries } = req.body ?? {};

  if (!isValidPeriodNumber(periodNumber)) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      error: `periodNumber must be a whole number from 1 to ${MAX_PERIOD}.`,
    });
  }
  const shapeError = validateShape(entries);
  if (shapeError) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: shapeError });
  }

  const anchorResult = await pool.query(
    'SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = $1',
    ['period_anchor_date']
  );
  const anchorDate = anchorResult.rows[0]?.value;
  if (!anchorDate) {
    return res.status(409).json({ error: 'Set the period anchor date first.' });
  }

  const current = periodForDate(anchorDate, todayIso());
  if (current === null) {
    return res.status(409).json({ error: 'Period 1 has not started yet.' });
  }
  if (periodNumber > current) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      error: `Period ${periodNumber} has not started yet. The current period is ${current}.`,
    });
  }

  const studentIds = [...new Set(entries.map((entry) => entry.studentId))];
  const [changesResult, parametersResult, studentsResult] = await Promise.all([
    pool.query(
      `SELECT parameter_id  AS "parameterId",
              period_number AS "periodNumber",
              scope,
              action
       FROM parameter_changes`
    ),
    pool.query(
      `SELECT id,
              name,
              min_value::float8 AS "minValue",
              max_value::float8 AS "maxValue"
       FROM parameters`
    ),
    pool.query(
      `SELECT id,
              full_name    AS "fullName",
              student_code AS "studentCode"
       FROM students
       WHERE id = ANY($1::uuid[]) AND deleted_at IS NULL`,
      [studentIds]
    ),
  ]);

  const usedIds = new Set(
    parametersUsedInPeriod(changesResult.rows, periodNumber)
  );
  const usedParameters = new Map(
    parametersResult.rows
      .filter((parameter) => usedIds.has(parameter.id))
      .map((parameter) => [parameter.id, parameter])
  );
  const studentsById = new Map(
    studentsResult.rows.map((student) => [student.id, student])
  );
  const knownStudentIds = new Set(studentsById.keys());

  const problems = findProblems({
    entries,
    usedParameters,
    knownStudentIds,
    periodNumber,
  });
  if (problems.length > 0) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      error: 'Some entries are invalid. Nothing was saved.',
      problems,
    });
  }

  const changes = [];
  const client = await pool.connect();
  try {
    await client.query('BEGIN');

    const existing = await client.query(
      `SELECT student_id   AS "studentId",
              parameter_id AS "parameterId",
              value::float8 AS value
       FROM evaluations
       WHERE period_number = $1 AND student_id = ANY($2::uuid[])`,
      [periodNumber, studentIds]
    );
    const before = new Map(
      existing.rows.map((row) => [
        `${row.studentId}:${row.parameterId}`,
        row.value,
      ])
    );

    for (const entry of entries) {
      const result =
        entry.value === null
          ? await client.query(
              `UPDATE evaluations
               SET value = NULL, updated_at = now()
               WHERE student_id = $1 AND parameter_id = $2
                 AND period_number = $3 AND value IS NOT NULL`,
              [entry.studentId, entry.parameterId, periodNumber]
            )
          : await client.query(
              `INSERT INTO evaluations
                 (student_id, parameter_id, period_number, value)
               VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
               ON CONFLICT (student_id, parameter_id, period_number)
               DO UPDATE SET value = EXCLUDED.value, updated_at = now()
               WHERE evaluations.value IS DISTINCT FROM EXCLUDED.value`,
              [entry.studentId, entry.parameterId, periodNumber, entry.value]
            );

      if (result.rowCount > 0) {
        const student = studentsById.get(entry.studentId);
        changes.push({
          studentId: entry.studentId,
          student: student.fullName,
          studentCode: student.studentCode,
          parameterId: entry.parameterId,
          parameter: usedParameters.get(entry.parameterId).name,
          from: before.get(`${entry.studentId}:${entry.parameterId}`) ?? null,
          to: entry.value,
        });
      }
    }

    if (changes.length > 0) {
      await recordAudit(client, {
        userId: req.user.id,
        actor: req.user.username,
        action: 'scores.save',
        entityType: 'period',
        entityId: String(periodNumber),
        details: { periodNumber, count: changes.length, changes },
      });
    }

    await client.query('COMMIT');
  } catch (err) {
    await client.query('ROLLBACK');
    throw err;
  } finally {
    client.release();
  }

  res.json({ periodNumber, changed: changes.length });
});

export default router;
What changed from before: the students query also returns names, the saving reads each cell's old value first, and it writes one log entry listing every cell that actually changed (old value, new value, and the student and parameter names at that moment). If saving fails, the log entry rolls back too.

F. The page of the log, admin only

Create src/routes/audit.js:

js
import { Router } from 'express';
import pool from '../db/pool.js';

const router = Router();

const CATEGORIES = [
  'student',
  'parameter',
  'parameter_change',
  'settings',
  'scores',
  'user',
  'auth',
  'api',
];
const PAGE_SIZE = 25;
const MAX_DETAIL_CHANGES = 50;

function toPositiveInt(value, fallback) {
  const number = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  return Number.isInteger(number) && number > 0 ? number : fallback;
}

router.get('/', async (req, res) => {
  const { category, actor } = req.query;
  const page = Math.min(toPositiveInt(req.query.page, 1), 100000);

  const conditions = [];
  const values = [];

  if (category !== undefined) {
    if (typeof category !== 'string' || !CATEGORIES.includes(category)) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Unknown category.' });
    }
    values.push(category);
    conditions.push(`split_part(a.action, '.', 1) = $${values.length}`);
  }
  if (actor !== undefined) {
    if (typeof actor !== 'string' || actor === '' || actor.length > 64) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid user filter.' });
    }
    values.push(actor.toLowerCase());
    conditions.push(`lower(a.actor) = $${values.length}`);
  }
  const where = conditions.length ? `WHERE ${conditions.join(' AND ')}` : '';

  const [countResult, listResult] = await Promise.all([
    pool.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS total FROM audit_log a ${where}`, values),
    pool.query(
      `SELECT a.id::text        AS id,
              a.occurred_at     AS "occurredAt",
              a.actor,
              a.action,
              a.entity_type     AS "entityType",
              a.entity_id       AS "entityId",
              a.details,
              s.full_name       AS "studentName",
              s.student_code    AS "studentCode",
              p.name            AS "parameterName",
              cp.name           AS "changeParameterName"
       FROM audit_log a
       LEFT JOIN students s
         ON a.entity_type = 'student' AND s.id::text = a.entity_id
       LEFT JOIN parameters p
         ON a.entity_type = 'parameter' AND p.id::text = a.entity_id
       LEFT JOIN parameters cp
         ON a.entity_type = 'parameter_change'
        AND cp.id::text = a.details->'result'->>'parameterId'
       ${where}
       ORDER BY a.occurred_at DESC, a.id DESC
       LIMIT ${PAGE_SIZE} OFFSET $${values.length + 1}`,
      [...values, (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE]
    ),
  ]);

  const entries = listResult.rows.map((row) => {
    const changes = row.details?.changes;
    if (Array.isArray(changes) && changes.length > MAX_DETAIL_CHANGES) {
      return {
        ...row,
        details: {
          ...row.details,
          changes: changes.slice(0, MAX_DETAIL_CHANGES),
          moreChanges: changes.length - MAX_DETAIL_CHANGES,
        },
      };
    }
    return row;
  });

  res.json({
    entries,
    total: countResult.rows[0].total,
    page,
    pageSize: PAGE_SIZE,
  });
});

export default router;
The list joins the students and parameters tables so a row can show the current name of what it refers to (for example a deleted student's name and code, which the log alone wouldn't have).
Long score lists are shortened to the first 50 cells for the page, with a "more" count.
The category and user filters are checked against a fixed list or a length limit, and all values travel as placeholders.

js
import connectPgSimple from 'connect-pg-simple';
import express from 'express';
import session from 'express-session';
import pool from './db/pool.js';
import { attachUser, authorize, requireLogin } from './middleware/auth.js';
import { auditRequests } from './middleware/audit.js';
import auditRouter from './routes/audit.js';
import authRouter from './routes/auth.js';
import evaluationsRouter from './routes/evaluations.js';
import parametersRouter from './routes/parameters.js';
import periodsRouter from './routes/periods.js';
import studentsRouter from './routes/students.js';

const secret = process.env.SESSION_SECRET;
if (!secret || secret.length < 32) {
  console.error(
    'SESSION_SECRET is missing or too short. Add a line like ' +
      'SESSION_SECRET=<64 random characters> to server/.env, then restart.'
  );
  process.exit(1);
}

const PgSession = connectPgSimple(session);
const app = express();
app.disable('x-powered-by');
app.use(express.json());

app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => {
  res.json({ ok: true });
});

app.use(
  session({
    store: new PgSession({ pool, tableName: 'user_sessions' }),
    name: 'sid',
    secret,
    resave: false,
    saveUninitialized: false,
    rolling: true,
    cookie: {
      httpOnly: true,
      sameSite: 'lax',
      secure: 'auto',
      maxAge: 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
    },
  })
);
app.use('/api', attachUser);
app.use('/api', auditRequests);

app.use('/api/auth', authRouter);
app.use('/api', requireLogin);

app.use(
  '/api/students',
  authorize({ GET: 'viewer', POST: 'staff', PATCH: 'staff', DELETE: 'admin' }),
  studentsRouter
);
app.use('/api/parameters', authorize({ GET: 'viewer', POST: 'admin', PATCH: 'admin' }));
app.use(
  '/api/parameter-changes',
  authorize({ GET: 'viewer', POST: 'admin', DELETE: 'admin' })
);
app.use('/api/periods', authorize({ GET: 'viewer' }));
app.use('/api/settings', authorize({ GET: 'viewer', PUT: 'admin' }));
app.use('/api/evaluations', authorize({ GET: 'viewer', PUT: 'staff' }));
app.use('/api/audit', authorize({ GET: 'admin' }), auditRouter);

app.use('/api', periodsRouter);
app.use('/api', parametersRouter);
app.use('/api', evaluationsRouter);

app.use('/api', (req, res) => {
  res.status(404).json({ error: 'Not found.' });
});

app.use((err, req, res, next) => {
  const status = err.status || 500;
  if (status >= 500) console.error(err);
  res.status(status).json({
    error: status >= 500 ? 'Something went wrong on the server.' : err.message,
  });
});

const port = process.env.PORT || 3000;
app.listen(port, () => {
  console.log(`Server running at http://localhost:${port}`);
});
The only differences from piece 20 are the two audit imports, the auditRequests line (placed early, so it sees everything), and the /api/audit route (admin only). The server restarts on save.

I. API and wording

Create src/api/audit.js:

js
import { request } from './http.js';

export function listAudit({ category = '', actor = '', page = 1 } = {}) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ page: String(page) });
  if (category) params.set('category', category);
  if (actor) params.set('actor', actor);
  return request(`/api/audit?${params}`);
}
Create src/lib/audit.js:

js
import { formatStudentCode } from './format.js';

export const CATEGORY_OPTIONS = [
  { value: '', label: 'Everything' },
  { value: 'scores', label: 'Scores' },
  { value: 'student', label: 'Students' },
  { value: 'parameter', label: 'Parameters' },
  { value: 'parameter_change', label: 'Parameter changes' },
  { value: 'settings', label: 'Settings' },
  { value: 'user', label: 'Users' },
  { value: 'auth', label: 'Sign-in and passwords' },
  { value: 'api', label: 'Other changes' },
];

function withCode(code) {
  return code ? ` (${formatStudentCode(code)})` : '';
}

export function describeAudit(entry) {
  const details = entry.details ?? {};
  const result = details.result ?? {};
  const request = details.request ?? {};

  switch (entry.action) {
    case 'student.create':
      return `Added student ${result.fullName ?? request.fullName ?? ''}${withCode(result.studentCode)}`.trim();
    case 'student.update':
      return `Renamed student to ${result.fullName ?? request.fullName ?? '?'}${withCode(result.studentCode)}`;
    case 'student.delete':
      return entry.studentName
        ? `Deleted student ${entry.studentName}${withCode(entry.studentCode)}`
        : 'Deleted a student';
    case 'parameter.create':
      return `Added parameter ${result.name ?? request.name ?? '?'}, used from Period ${request.startPeriod ?? '?'} on`;
    case 'parameter.update':
      return `Edited parameter ${entry.parameterName ?? result.name ?? 'a parameter'}`;
    case 'parameter_change.create': {
      const name = entry.changeParameterName ?? 'a parameter';
      const verb = result.action === 'add' ? 'Added' : 'Removed';
      const where =
        result.scope === 'this_period_only'
          ? 'for this period only'
          : 'from this period on';
      return `${verb} ${name} ${where}, Period ${result.periodNumber ?? '?'}`;
    }
    case 'parameter_change.delete':
      return 'Undid a parameter change';
    case 'settings.period_anchor':
      return `Set the period start date to ${result.anchorDate ?? request.anchorDate ?? '?'}`;
    case 'scores.save': {
      const count = details.count ?? 0;
      return `Saved ${count} score${count === 1 ? '' : 's'} in Period ${details.periodNumber ?? '?'}`;
    }
    case 'auth.login':
      return 'Signed in';
    case 'auth.login_failed':
      return `Failed sign-in attempt for "${details.attemptedUsername ?? '?'}"`;
    case 'auth.logout':
      return 'Signed out';
    case 'auth.password_change':
      return 'Changed their password';
    case 'api.change':
      return `${details.method ?? ''} ${details.path ?? ''}`.trim();
    default:
      return entry.action;
  }
}
The server keeps raw facts, and this function turns each into a readable sentence. Unknown actions just show their raw name, so new kinds of entries never break the page. Piece 23 adds the wording for user changes.

Create test/audit.test.js:

js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { describeAudit } from '../src/lib/audit.js';

test('describes student events', () => {
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({
      action: 'student.create',
      details: { result: { fullName: 'Maria Lopez', studentCode: 'K7M2QX9A' } },
    }),
    'Added student Maria Lopez (K7M2-QX9A)'
  );
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({
      action: 'student.delete',
      studentName: 'Maria Lopez',
      studentCode: 'K7M2QX9A',
      details: {},
    }),
    'Deleted student Maria Lopez (K7M2-QX9A)'
  );
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({ action: 'student.delete', details: {} }),
    'Deleted a student'
  );
});

test('describes parameter changes', () => {
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({
      action: 'parameter_change.create',
      changeParameterName: 'Balance',
      details: {
        result: { action: 'remove', scope: 'this_period_only', periodNumber: 7 },
      },
    }),
    'Removed Balance for this period only, Period 7'
  );
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({
      action: 'parameter_change.create',
      changeParameterName: 'Balance',
      details: {
        result: { action: 'add', scope: 'from_here_on', periodNumber: 5 },
      },
    }),
    'Added Balance from this period on, Period 5'
  );
});

test('describes score saves with singular and plural', () => {
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({ action: 'scores.save', details: { count: 3, periodNumber: 12 } }),
    'Saved 3 scores in Period 12'
  );
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({ action: 'scores.save', details: { count: 1, periodNumber: 12 } }),
    'Saved 1 score in Period 12'
  );
});

test('describes sign-in events', () => {
  assert.equal(describeAudit({ action: 'auth.login', details: {} }), 'Signed in');
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({
      action: 'auth.login_failed',
      details: { attemptedUsername: 'admin' },
    }),
    'Failed sign-in attempt for "admin"'
  );
});

test('unknown actions and generic changes still produce text', () => {
  assert.equal(describeAudit({ action: 'something.new' }), 'something.new');
  assert.equal(
    describeAudit({
      action: 'api.change',
      details: { method: 'POST', path: '/api/x' },
    }),
    'POST /api/x'
  );
});

You should see 5 new green tests, for a total of pass 25 and fail 0 (20 + 5).

J. The page

Create src/pages/AuditPage.jsx:

jsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { keepPreviousData, useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { listAudit } from '../api/audit.js';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthContext.jsx';
import { CATEGORY_OPTIONS, describeAudit } from '../lib/audit.js';
import { formatDateTime, formatStudentCode } from '../lib/format.js';
import { useDebouncedValue } from '../lib/useDebouncedValue.js';

function Details({ entry }) {
  const details = entry.details ?? {};

  if (entry.action === 'scores.save' && Array.isArray(details.changes)) {
    return (
      <>
        <table className="audit-changes">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Student</th>
              <th>Parameter</th>
              <th className="num">From</th>
              <th className="num">To</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {details.changes.map((change) => (
              <tr key={`${change.studentId}:${change.parameterId}`}>
                <td>
                  {change.student} ({formatStudentCode(change.studentCode)})
                </td>
                <td>{change.parameter}</td>
                <td className="num">{change.from ?? '—'}</td>
                <td className="num">{change.to ?? 'cleared'}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        {details.moreChanges > 0 && (
          <p className="hint">…and {details.moreChanges} more changes.</p>
        )}
      </>
    );
  }
  return <pre>{JSON.stringify(details, null, 2)}</pre>;
}

function AuditPage() {
  const { can } = useAuth();
  const [category, setCategory] = useState('');
  const [actorText, setActorText] = useState('');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const actor = useDebouncedValue(actorText.trim(), 300);

  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['audit', { category, actor, page }],
    queryFn: () => listAudit({ category, actor, page }),
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
    enabled: can('admin'),
  });

  if (!can('admin')) {
    return (
      <main>
        <h1>Audit log</h1>
        <p>Only administrators can see the audit log.</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  const { data } = query;
  const totalPages = data ? Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.total / data.pageSize)) : 1;

  return (
    <main className="wide">
      <h1>Audit log</h1>
      <p className="hint">
        A record of who changed what, and when. Passwords are never recorded.
      </p>

      <div className="filters">
        <select
          aria-label="Category"
          value={category}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setCategory(event.target.value);
            setPage(1);
          }}
        >
          {CATEGORY_OPTIONS.map((option) => (
            <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
              {option.label}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <input
          value={actorText}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setActorText(event.target.value);
            setPage(1);
          }}
          placeholder="Filter by username"
          aria-label="Filter by username"
          autoComplete="off"
        />
        <button type="button" onClick={() => query.refetch()}>
          Refresh
        </button>
      </div>

      {query.isPending && <p>Loading...</p>}
      {query.isError && <p className="error">{query.error.message}</p>}
      {data && data.entries.length === 0 && <p>Nothing recorded yet.</p>}
      {data && data.entries.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <div className="table-wrap">
            <table className={query.isPlaceholderData ? 'stale' : undefined}>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>When</th>
                  <th>Who</th>
                  <th>What</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {data.entries.map((entry) => (
                  <tr key={entry.id}>
                    <td className="nowrap">{formatDateTime(entry.occurredAt)}</td>
                    <td>{entry.actor}</td>
                    <td>
                      {describeAudit(entry)}
                      <details className="audit-details">
                        <summary>Details</summary>
                        <Details entry={entry} />
                      </details>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <div className="pager">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setPage(page - 1)}
              disabled={page <= 1}
            >
              Previous
            </button>
            <span>
              Page {page} of {totalPages} ({data.total} entries)
            </span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setPage(page + 1)}
              disabled={page >= totalPages}
            >
              Next
            </button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

export default AuditPage;

Run and test
Start the server and client and sign in as admin. Then:

There's a new Audit log link. Open it. You see your earlier sign-ins and any changes from the server tests.
Do a few things in the app: add a student, rename them, delete them, add a parameter, change a parameter's unit, and save some scores on Evaluate. Press Refresh on the audit page. Each shows up as a readable sentence, such as "Renamed student to ... (K7M2-QX9A)" or "Saved 3 scores in Period 12".
Click Details on a score entry. You see a table with each cell's student, parameter, and From → To. Change the same score again and save, and the new entry shows the old value as From.
Delete a student, then look at that entry. The name and code still show, because the page looks them up.
Filter by Scores, then Students, and type admin in the username box. The list narrows, and page numbers reset.
Sign out, then try to sign in with a wrong password twice. Sign in properly and check that "Failed sign-in attempt for ..." appears twice.
Sign in as viewer1. There's no Audit log link. Typing /audit shows "Only administrators can see the audit log."
In the terminal, confirm the server refuses a viewer too:
api -c /tmp/viewer.jar -X POST http://localhost:3000/api/auth/login -d '{"username":"viewer1","password":"YOUR-VIEWER-PASSWORD"}'
api -b /tmp/viewer.jar http://localhost:3000/api/audit
Expected: the second gives 403. (The viewer's password is the one you set earlier. If viewer1 still has a temporary password from piece 20, you may need the shortcut from there.)
9. Stop the server, and check that saving scores in the browser fails cleanly. This shows nothing half-written. Start the server again.

If something goes wrong

The audit page is empty after real changes: check the server console for "Could not write audit entry". If you see it, send me the error.
relation "audit_log" does not exist: run npm run migrate in server/.
The password check prints something other than 0: stop and send me the full result, because that would mean a secret was logged.
A score save now fails: evaluations.js wasn't replaced completely, so re-copy it and check the server console.
"Only administrators" appears for admin: the sign-in expired or the role isn't admin, so sign out and in again.
A test fails: send me the failing output.
What to send back

The npm test summaries (server and client)
The password check result (should be 0)
A screenshot of the audit page with a score entry expanded, or a description
The git status output
Next is piece 23: the Users page. Admins create accounts (with a one-time temporary password), change roles, deactivate people, and reset passwords, with safeguards so the last administrator can never be locked out. Every action lands in the audit log.
